## Built features

Each feature: its screen, the routes the screen (and the modules it imports) calls, with the permission in brackets and the route's
file and line after the arrow, then the tables each route's handler names. A handler that only delegates to a service is marked
`(file)` and shows the tables of its whole route file and the services it imports. Calls made by the shared shell
(`web/src/lib`: session, bootstrap, pagers) are left out of every feature. Table lists are read statically from the
SQL a handler and the module-level helpers it names contain, so a helper shared by several handlers can add its tables to each.
